Um, well... I mean, I really try to do a good job. I don't want to sound like I'm fishing for compliments or anything, but my team is usually pretty happy with my work. 

But... I guess, sometimes, I do have these thoughts. Like... well, I built this dashboard for my department a while ago, and people still bring it up and say nice things about it. And I know I should just say thank you, but... my first thought is always that I somehow just fooled them into thinking it's good, or that I just got lucky. 

And then, if I miss a follow-up or send an email a little late... it feels like, I don't know, like it's proof that I'm not actually as capable as everyone thinks I am. That they're finally going to realize I'm not good enough. 

I know it's probably silly to think that way, and I really try not to let it affect my work. I hope it doesn't make me sound incompetent? I just... yeah, I guess I do feel like I'm failing sometimes, even when I'm trying my hardest. Is that normal to feel that way?

Um, well... I try to stay as active as I can. I mean, I'm only 22, so I feel like I should have plenty of energy, right? I don't want to sound lazy or anything. I really do try to push through it.

But... I guess, yeah, I have been feeling pretty tired lately. It's just... by the afternoon, I'm just so wiped out. Even when it's been a totally normal day, not super stressful or anything. Just doing simple things, like fixing a spreadsheet error or finishing up some expense paperwork... it feels like it takes everything I have. 

And I usually play soccer, but lately, just getting myself to go feels like... I don't know, like I'm dragging my body and my brain through mud. I still go most of the time, because I don't want to let my team down, but it's just really hard. 

I know everyone gets tired after work, so maybe I'm just not managing my time well or something? I hope it doesn't sound like I'm just complaining about normal stuff. Is it okay that I feel that way?

Like, no matter how much effort I put in, it just won't happen. \n\nI'm sure it's just a phase, though, you know? Everyone gets a little discouraged sometimes. Does that make sense? Like, even when things are going perfectly fine at the office, or when people are being really nice to me, I still just feel sort of down. Or empty, I guess. It's actually why I finally decided to come in, to be honest. Um, I think... well, like I was kind of trying to say just a minute ago, I definitely wouldn't call them chores. That makes it sound like I'm complaining about having to do them, and I really try not to be a complainer! I mean, when I finish a tough book, I still feel good about getting through it. I hope that's the kind of answer you were looking for? I just don't want to make it sound worse than it is.\n\nDoctor: Can you describe what your mood is like on most days? Do you feel down or hopeless most of the time, even when things seem to be going okay?\n\nPatient: Um, well... I guess I wouldn't say I feel down all the time, exactly. Like I said, things are okay at work, and I try really hard to keep a positive attitude about it. I don't want to sound like I'm complaining. \n\nBut... maybe there is a little bit of, um, I don't know if \"hopeless\" is the right word, but... well, I used to be really driven. I don't know, I look at those same plans and I just kind of assume I'm not going to get there. Like, no matter how much effort I put in, it just won't happen. \n\nI'm sure it's just a phase, though, you know? Everyone gets a little discouraged sometimes. Does that make sense? I hope I'm not making it sound too dramatic.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that most days, you feel this low or empty mood, even if it's not extremely intense?\n\nPatient: Um, yeah... I guess that's a pretty good way to describe it. It's not like I'm crying all the time or anything like that. I really do try to keep a smile on my face, especially at work. \n\nBut... yeah, I think I'd say that most days, there's just this... flat, kind of heavy feeling? It's actually why I finally decided to come in, to be honest. I just want to make sure I'm giving you the right information.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself doing things more slowly, or feeling like it's harder to get motivated to do even small tasks?\n\nPatient: Um, well... I just... I haven't really been paying super close attention to it, but I'm pretty sure everything is normal there. I don't think so? I mean, I still get all my reports done on time at work, and I try really hard to stay on top of things. I wouldn't want my boss to think I'm slacking off or anything. \n\nI guess sometimes getting started on a project takes a little more effort than it used to, but I think everyone procrastinates a bit, right? I definitely don't feel like I'm moving in slow motion or anything like that. I still get up and get dressed and go to work every day. I hope that's okay? I just wouldn't say I'm moving slower, really.\n\nDoctor: Have you found yourself feeling like you're not good enough, or that you're failing even when you're doing your best?\n\nPatient: Um, well... I mean, I really try to do a good job. I don't want to sound like I'm fishing for compliments or anything, but my team is usually pretty happy with my work. \n\nBut... I guess, sometimes, I do have these thoughts. Like... well, I built this dashboard for my department a while ago, and people still bring it up and say nice things about it. And I know I should just say thank you, but... my first thought is always that I somehow just fooled them into thinking it's good, or that I just got lucky. \n\nAnd then, if I miss a follow-up or send an email a little late... it feels like, I don't know, like it's proof that I'm not actually as capable as everyone thinks I am. That they're finally going to realize I'm not good enough. \n\nI know it's probably silly to think that way, and I really try not to let it affect my work. I hope it doesn't make me sound incompetent? I just... yeah, I guess I do feel like I'm failing sometimes, even when I'm trying my hardest. Is that normal to feel that way?\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that you feel tired or that you don’t have much energy, even when you haven’t done anything physically or mentally exhausting?\n\nPatient: Um, well... I hope it doesn't sound like I'm just complaining about normal stuff. Is it okay that I feel that way?\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite or weight lately—like eating more or less than usual, or gaining or losing weight without trying?\n\nPatient: Um, well... Um, I'm sorry, I feel like I'm maybe not explaining myself very well today, since we keep coming back to this. I really don't mean to be difficult or confusing!

I guess, yeah, I have been feeling pretty tired lately. It's just... by the afternoon, I'm just so wiped out. Even when it's been a totally normal day, not super stressful or anything.
